# UI ledger: pages and elements

Generated by `build_ledger.py` from `parts/*.json`. Do not edit by hand; edit the part files and rebuild.

335 pages, 1720 interactive elements.

Legend: **Action -> target** uses the ledger action vocabulary; a target in `code` is a page id in this file. `!` marks destructive elements, `~` marks host wiring of a control that is also listed on an `embedded-*` page (see README).

## Shell and navigation

### system

**[System entry points: share, launcher shortcuts, links, notifications]** · overlay · `_OcAppState` · `lib/main.dart:259`

Non-UI navigation handled by OcApp: each element is one external trigger and where it lands. Nothing is sent on the user's behalf; an undeliverable trigger is consumed with a snackbar notice and usually opens '/servers' (_showServersForLaunch, skipped when '/servers' is already on top).

### chat-draft-attachment-recovery-sheet

**Some attachments need attention** · sheet · `_recoverDraftAttachments` · `lib/ui/screens/chat_screen.dart:724`

Asks whether to continue with only the recoverable saved draft attachments when some are missing, unreadable or belong to another project.

Gates: `!_conn.isIsolated`; `recovered.unavailable.isNotEmpty`; `_draftLocation == _conn.locationRevision`

Reached from: `system`

| Label | Type | Action -> target | Effect | Gates | Line |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Use available attachments ! | button | mutate | Replaces _attachments with recovered.attachments, clears _draftRecoveryBlocked and _draftSaveFailure and schedules _persistDraft(), which drops the unavailable attachments from the saved draft (FilledButton rendered by showConfirmSheet in lib/ui/widgets/confirm_sheet.dart; pops true). |  | 730 |
| Keep saved draft | button | dismiss | Keeps the stored draft snapshot untouched; _draftRecoveryBlocked stays true and _draftSaveFailure becomes SessionDraftFailure.attachments (cancel button of showConfirmSheet; pops false). |  | 731 |
